Potential algorithmic ideas:
1. Use a more accurate objective: Instead of soft log-sum-exp, use exact max plus subgradient, but optimize with nonsmooth techniques (bundle method). Since n=6121, FFT exact cheap. The soft approximation may be biased; exact subgradient can find better. However max of smooth quadratics is convex? Is B_k(a)=∑_{i+j=k}a_i a_j. Each B_k is convex? Hessian has rank? For fixed k, Hessian with entries 2 at (i,k-i) and (k-i,i) (if i≠k-i), zero diag except if i=k/2? Hessian not PSD? Let's test k=2: B2=a0a2+a1^2+a2a0 =2a0a2+a1^2. Hessian [[0,0,1],[0,2,0],[1,0,0]] eigenvalues ±1,0 -> indefinite. So not convex. Soft may work.

2. Use local search with coordinate descents on pairwise interactions. Since B_k gradient at i depends on a_{k-i}. We can perform exact pairwise updates for pairs (i,j) with i+j around max: adjust a_i,a_j to reduce max B while preserving sum. This could polish.

3. Use random restarts with different n (length). Larger n maybe lower score approaching continuous optimum. Previous n=6121. We can try n around 10000 or 20000? Compute budget: FFT length ~ next power 2 for 2n. n=10000 L=32768, convolve cost ~ few ms? Python numpy FFT 32k ~ maybe 1-2 ms per forward+inverse? Function `fourier_prior(n, rng, max_amp=1.2, K=6)`:
   t=np.arange(n)/n
   amps sorted decreasing: base amplitude maybe rng.uniform(0.2,0.8)/sqrt(k). Sum. Add noise. Return normalized exp(z).
But to get asymmetry, include linear trend: `a*t`? Use transformed `x = (i/(n-1) - 0.5)`. Add terms: linear, quadratic, sin 2πk with random phase, especially sine fundamental to break symmetry. Use constraints nonnegative by exp. To allow near zeros, amplitude can be >2. But for sum distribution, high amplitude may create peaks. Random in [-2,2] low freq.
Pseudo:
   z = rng.normal(0,0.1,n)
   z += amp1 * (t-0.5)
   z += amp2 * (t-0.5)**2
   for k in 1..6: amp = rng.uniform(0.05,0.8)/sqrt(k); ph=rng.uniform(0,2π); z+=amp*cos(2π k t+ph)
Return normalize(exp(z - max)).
Also generate "anti-symmetric sine" p=1+ c sin(2πx) + d sin(4πx) via random c within nonneg amplitude. But exp prior covers.

Optimizer `optimize_vector(a, ctx, rng, time_limit, initial_q=4)`:
   ctx: n,L,m.
   deadline local.
   a=normalize(a)
   best_score=score_fft(a)
   best_a=a.copy(); current=a.copy()
   eta=0.2; beta=0.5; q=4; no_accept=0
   while time < deadline:
      # compute B, maxB, score
      B=conv_self(current)
      maxB = max(B)
      score = 2*n*maxB (sum=1)
      if score < best_score: update best
      # weights
      M=maxB+1e-300
      r = B/M
      # For q, if B/M small and q large, underflow; okay.
      w = r ** q
      s = w.sum(); if s<=0 w uniform
      w = w/s
      # mix uniform maybe if q large: w = (1-alpha)*w + alpha/m ones? alpha=0.02 if q>=8 else 0.
      if q >= 8: w += 0.02/m; w /= w.sum()
      g = conv_w_a(w, current); if max zero break
      # normalize gradient
      gs = np.median(g[g>0]) or mean
      # trial directions
      trials=[]
      # multiplicative steps
      for eta_t in [eta, 0.5*eta, 2*eta?] maybe if no accept.
         x = np.log(current + 1e-300)
         x -= eta_t * (g/gs)
         x -= x.max()
         y = np.exp(x); y /= y.sum()
         trials.append(y)
      # inverse gradient beta
      inv = gs / np.maximum(g, 1e-300)
      for beta_t in [0.5,1.0]:
         y = current * (np.maximum(inv, 0.0?)**?)? If g zero, inv huge -> push edges; cap inv to e.g. 1e6.
         y = current * np.minimum((gs/g_safe)**beta_t, 1e6)
         normalize; trials.append
      # line search each: compute score_fft(y), keep min
      if trial score < current_score: accept, update current; reset no_accept; adapt eta up if improved with large eta.
      else no_accept++; eta*=0.7; q maybe increase.
      if no_accept > 30:
         # try CG candidate
         y = cg_inverse_candidate(current,w,ctx,iters=20)
         if score improves accept.
         # random reheat: mix with random prior
         prior = fourier_prior(n,rng)
         mix=0.2+0.3*rng.random()
         current=normalize((1-mix)*best_a + mix*prior) # maybe current? Use best for recovery.
         q=2; eta=0.2; no_accept=0
      # periodic re-evaluate best and maybe swap current to best after long
      if iterations%100==0 and current_score not improve? current=best_a.copy(); decrease q? etc.
   reoptimize best briefly? Return best_a,best_score.

Let's detail functions:

`_ctx(n)`:
   m=2*n-1
   L=1
   while L < 3*n-2: L*=2
   return n,L,m

`_normalize(a, floor=0.0)`:
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   a=where(isfinite, a,0); a=max(0,a)
   s=a.sum(); if not finite or s<=0: random? return ones/n?
   a=a/s
   if floor>0: a=np.maximum(a,floor); a/=a.sum()
   return a

Need avoid zero floor causing log -inf. Use floor=1e-300? For optimizer, use floor=1e-18 maybe; shape unaffected tiny. But after normalize with floor, all positive. We can use floor=1e-15. Score unaffected. For final, we can remove tiny? zeros allowed; keep positive.

`_conv_self(a, L, m)`:
   f=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(f*f, L)[:m]
   c=np.maximum(c,0)
   # numerical sum may deviate; normalize? Since a sum1, c sum ~1; clipping adds small. We can renormalize c? For weights, no. For max, clipping negative only.
   return c

`_score_norm(a)`: B=_conv_self; max; return 2n*max (since sum normalized). But if a not normalized, use sum. In optimizer a normalized. Could compute `s=a.sum(); return 2*n*maxB/(s*s)`. Use for safety.

`_conv_grad(w, a, L, m, n, clip=True)`:
   a_rev=a[::-1]
   W=np.fft.rfft(w, L)
   Af=np.fft.rfft(a_rev, L)
   g=np.fft.irfft(W*Af, L)[n-1:2*n-1]
   if clip: g=np.maximum(g,0)
   return g

Pseudo:
```
def _optimize(a, n,L,m, rng, limit):
    a=_normalize(a, 1e-15)
    B=_conv_self(a,L,m); mx=B.max(); best_score=2*n*mx
    best_a=a.copy(); cur=a.copy()
    cur_score=best_score
    deadline=time.monotonic()+limit
    eta=0.15; q=2; no=0; it=0; last_best=...
    history? 
    while time < deadline:
       if time > deadline - 1: break
       B=_conv_self(cur,L,m)
       mx=B.max(); cur_score=2*n*mx
       if cur_score < best_score:
           best_score=cur_score; best_a=cur.copy(); no=0; recent_best=time
       # if no improvement for 120s, restart from best with new q/eta? maybe.
       if time - last_improve > 150: ...
       M=mx+eps
       r=B/M
       # choose q: if no_accept small q start else etc
       q_use = int(q)
       with np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore', divide='ignore'):
           u=np.power(r, q_use)
       u[~np.isfinite]=0; s=u.sum()
       if s<=0: u=np.ones(m)/m
       else: u/=s
       if q_use >= 4:
           u = 0.95*u + 0.05/m
           u /= u.sum()
       g=_conv_grad(u, cur, L,m,n, clip=True)
       gs = np.sum(g); if <=0: break
       # maybe scale by max for better: scale = max(g) or mean.
       gmean=gs/n
       # generate candidates
       cands=[]
       # multiplicative with eta adaptive
       for e in (eta, 0.5*eta):
           x=np.log(cur+1e-300)
           x = x - e * (g/gmean)
           x -= x.max()
           y=np.exp(x); ysum=y.sum(); if finite and >0: y/=ysum; cands.append(y)
       # inverse gradient
       inv=np.empty(n); gm=np.maximum(g,1e-300); np.divide(gmean, gm, out=inv); np.clip(inv,1e-6,1e6, out=inv)
       for b in (0.35,0.7):
           factor=np.power(inv,b)
           y=cur*factor; ysum=y.sum(); ...
       # optional small noise
       y=cur*(1+0.01*rng.normal(0,1,n)); normalize.
       # evaluate candidates: we can compute score_fft for each; to save, if current_score already high, maybe just first.
       best_cand=cur; best_c_score=cur_score
       for y in cands:
           sc=_score_norm(y)
           if sc < best_c_score - 1e-14:
              best_c_score=sc; best_cand=y
       if best_c_score < cur_score - 1e-14:
           cur=best_cand; cur_score=best_c_score; no=0; eta=min(0.6, eta*1.15)
       else:
           no+=1; eta=max(0.02, eta*0.75)
           if no > 20:
              # cg candidate
              y=_cg_candidate(cur,u,n,L,m,20)
              sc=_score_norm(y)
              if sc < cur_score -1e-14: cur=...
              else:
                 # reheat from best
                 mix=0.2+0.4*rng.random()
                 prior=_fourier_prior(n,rng)
                 cur=_normalize((1-mix)*best_a + mix*prior,1e-15)
                 q=2; eta=0.15; no=0
           if no > 60:
              # force reheat more
       q = 2 + min(30, int(no//3))? Or schedule.
       it++
       if it%200==0: q=2? 
```

CG candidate function:
```
def _cg_candidate(a,w,n,L,m,iters=20):
    x=a.copy()
    Hx=_conv_grad(w,x,L,m,n,clip=False)
    r=np.ones(n)-Hx
    p=r.copy()
    rho=np.dot(r,r)
    if rho <=0: return a
    for _ in range(iters):
       y=_conv_grad(w,p,L,m,n,clip=False)
       py=np.dot(p,y)
       if py <=1e-300 or not finite: break
       alpha=rho/py
       x = x + alpha*p
       r = r - alpha*y
       rho_new=np.dot(r,r)
       if not finite or rho_new <1e-30: break
       beta=rho_new/rho
       p = r + beta*p
       rho=rho_new
    y=np.maximum(x,0.0)
    s=y.sum()
    if s<=0: return a
    y/=s
    # mix with a to reduce disruption
    return _normalize(0.5*y+0.5*a,1e-15)
```
But if x large/negative, CG may blow. Limit alpha? Could check finite. Stop if norm x huge. Use 12 iters.

Maybe H matvec using w from current B. w sum1; H maps ones? The target equation Hx=1 may yield small solution because w sum1 (entries average 1/(2n)), H row sums maybe O(n/m)=O(1/2). To get Hx=1, x large. Maybe scale target to `target = row_sum`? Since we only need gradient constant, not 1. Actually condition H a = λ 1. λ can be chosen. For current a, H a = g. We want x such that H x is proportional to 1. Solving Hx=1 gives λ fixed. But scaling x by 2 gives Hx=2, also constant. So scale irrelevant. Equation Hx=1 just a scaling. Good. But if H row sums small, x large. CG initial r=1-Hx; okay.

Maybe solve Hx = mean row? Not needed.

```
def _spectral_flatten(a, iters=10):
  for _ in iters:
    B=conv_self(a)
    # target = min(max(B, 0.9*max(B)?), max?)? Actually to flatten, target = B + γ*(median - B) for B>median? Use T = clip(B, 0, median + 0.5*(max-median))
```

```
def _next_pow2(x):
    if x <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()
```

`_normalize(a, floor=0.0, rng=None)`:
Need if all zero and rng available? If no, ones.
```
a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
s=float(a.sum())
if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
    a=np.ones(len(a), dtype=np.float64)
    s=float(n)
a = a/s
if floor > 0:
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    a /= a.sum()
return a
```

`_fft_conv_self(a, L, m)`:
```
f=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
c=np.fft.irfft(f*f, L)[:m]
c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
return c
```
Potential small imaginary discarded by irfft. Good.

`_gradient_ctx(w,a,ctx, clip=True)`:
```

W=np.fft.rfft(w,L); Af=np.fft.rfft(a_rev,L)
g=np.fft.irfft(W*Af,L)[n-1:2*n-1]
g=np.where(np.isfinite(g),g,0.0)
if clip: np.maximum(g,0,out=g)
return g
```

`_fourier_prior(n, rng, style=0)`:
```
t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
x=2*t-1
z=np.zeros(n)
z += rng.normal(0,0.08)
z += rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0)*x
z += rng.uniform(-0.6,0.6)*(x*x-1/3)
kmax=int(rng.integers(2,8))
for k in range(1,kmax+1):
    amp=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.6)/math.sqrt(k))
    ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    z += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*t+ph)
if style==1:
    z += float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.2))*np.sin(2*np.pi*t)
elif style==2:
    z += float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.2))*np.sin(4*np.pi*t)
z -= np.max(z)
p=np.exp(z)
p=np.maximum(p,1e-300)
return _normalize(p, floor=1e-15)
```

Maybe include target B spectral candidates from uniform/param? We'll define `_spectral_candidate(a, ctx, rng, iters=3)`.

```
def _spectral_candidate(a, ctx, rng, iters=4):
    n,L,m=ctx
    a=_normalize(a,1e-15)
    for _ in range(iters):
        B=_fft_conv_self(a,L,m)
        mx=B.max()
        if mx <=0: break
        med=float(np.median(B))
        gamma=float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.8))
        T=(1-gamma)*B + gamma*med
        # ensure endpoints maybe not too high? T = np.maximum(T,0)
        # optional clip above target: T = np.minimum(T, med + rng.uniform(0.2,0.5)*(mx-med))
        clip_hi = med + float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.7))*max(1e-12,mx-med)
        T = np.minimum(T, clip_hi)
        T = np.maximum(T,0)
        ss=T.sum()
        if ss<=0: break
        T/=ss
        # full FFT sqrt
        zero = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        zero[:m]=T
        Hc=np.fft.fft(zero)
        # Handle principal sqrt with Hermitian enforcement
        R=np.sqrt(Hc)
        R[0]=complex(float(np.real(R[0])),0.0)
        half=L//2
        if half < L:
            R[half]=complex(float(np.real(R[half])),0.0)
        for k in range(1, half if L%2 else half):
            R[L-k]=np.conj(R[k])
        # if L even, k=half already conjugate real; set.
        A=np.fft.ifft(R)[:n].real
        A=np.where(np.isfinite(A),A,0)
        A=np.maximum(A,0)
        # If all zero, fallback
        if float(A.sum()) <= 1e-200:
            A=a
        a=_normalize(A,1e-15)
    return a
```
But full FFT length 65536 with ifft for each spectral candidate cost; maybe few. Loop 4* candidate maybe ok.
The Hermitian enforcement loop 32768 in Python per spectral candidate! That is expensive. Avoid. Use rfft sqrt? For rfft, irfft assumes Hermitian automatically. We can compute `Rr=np.sqrt(np.fft.rfft(T,L))` and `A=np.fft.irfft(Rr,L)[:n].real`. But sqrt principal for rfft does not ensure H0/Hhalf real? `irfft` expects rfft spectrum; if Rr not satisfying `Rr[0]`, `Rr[-1]` real, irfft arbitrary but real output still (it treats last as Nyquist real part? Actually `irfft` assumes input with real even symmetry; if imaginary at Nyquist, it ignores? It expects last element real, but complex allowed? It returns real using formula, imaginary Nyquist may affect? Better set Rr[0]=real, Rr[-1]=real. This is cheap.
```
Rr=np.sqrt(np.fft.rfft(T,L))
Rr[0]=complex(float(np.real(Rr[0])),0)
Rr[-1]=complex(float(np.real(Rr[-1])),0)
A=np.fft.irfft(Rr,L)[:n].real
```
This should output real (irfft). It may not be consistent with negative frequencies if angle wrap, but okay. Use this.
No Python loop.

Include spectral candidates from a few random/param initial. Maybe 10.

Pseudo more concrete:
```
def _optimize_ctx(a, ctx, rng, limit, verbose=False):
    n,L,m=ctx
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(1.0,limit)
    a=_normalize(a,1e-15)
    cur=a.copy()
    cur_score=_score_ctx(cur,ctx)
    best_a=cur.copy(); best_score=cur_score
    eta=0.12
    no_accept=0
    it=0
    last_best=start
    q_cycle=[1,1,2,2,2,4,4,6,8,8,16]
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
       B=_fft_conv_self(cur,L,m)
       mx=float(B.max())
       if not finite: break
       cur_score=float(2.0*n*mx) # cur normalized
       if cur_score < best_score - 1e-14:
           best_score=cur_score; best_a=cur.copy(); last_best=time.monotonic(); no_accept=0
       # weight
       M=mx if mx>0 else 1
       r=B/M
       q_idx = min(len(q_cycle)-1, it//30 + min(6, no_accept//8))
       q = q_cycle[q_idx]
       if rng.random() < 0.15:
           q = int(rng.choice([1,2,4,8,16,32])) # no lambda; use indexing
       with np.errstate(...):
           u=np.power(r,q)
       u=np.where(np.isfinite(u),u,0)
       su=u.sum()
       if su <=0: u=np.ones(m,m)/m
       else: u/=su
       # add background and hard
       alpha_bg = 0.05 if q>=4 else 0.02
       u = (1-alpha_bg)*u + alpha_bg/m
       if q>=8:
          thr = 0.90
          mask = r >= thr
          cnt=int(mask.sum())
          if cnt > 0 and cnt < m//2:
             hv=np.zeros(m); hv[mask]=1.0; hv/=float(cnt)
             u = 0.4*hv + 0.6*u
             u /= u.sum()
          elif cnt >0:
             u = 0.2*hv+0.8*u; u/=u.sum()
       g=_gradient_ctx(u,cur,ctx,True)
       gs=float(u.size? no g mean)
       gm=np.mean(g)
       if gm <=0 or not finite: break
       trials=0; best_trial=cur_score; best_y=cur
       # 1 multiplicative
       for e in (eta, 0.5*eta):
           x=np.log(cur+1e-300)
           x = x - e * (g/gm)
           x -= np.max(x)
           y=np.exp(x)
           ys=float(y.sum())
           if ys > 0 and finite:
               y/=ys
               sc=_score_ctx(y,ctx)
               if sc < best_trial - 1e-14:
                   best_trial=sc; best_y=y; trials+=1
       # inverse gradient
       inv=np.empty(n)
       gsmax=np.maximum(g,1e-300)
       np.divide(gm, gsmax, out=inv)
       np.clip(inv,1e-8,1e8,out=inv)
       for beta in (0.4,0.8):
           factor=np.power(inv,beta)
           y=cur*factor
           ys=y.sum(); if finite>0: y/=ys; sc=...
       # random small perturbation if no trial improved
       if best_trial >= cur_score - 1e-14 and rng.random()<0.3:
           y=cur*(1.0 + 0.05*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n))
           y=np.maximum(y,0); ys=y.sum(); ...
       if best_trial < cur_score - 1e-14:
           cur=best_y; cur_score=best_trial; no_accept=0; eta=min(0.5, eta*1.12)
       else:
           no_accept +=1
           eta=max(0.02, eta*0.8)
           # occasional CG
           if no_accept == 20 or no_accept == 40:
               y=_cg_candidate(cur,u,n,L,m,12)
               sc=_score_ctx(y,ctx)
               if sc < cur_score -1e-14: cur=...
           if no_accept >= 50:
               # reheat from best or random
               mix=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.6))
               if rng.random() <0.5:
                   prior=_fourier_prior(n,rng, style=int(rng.integers(0,3)))
               else:
                   prior=_param_candidates? too heavy; use fourier.
               cur=_normalize((1-mix)*best_a + mix*prior,1e-15)
               # maybe run spectral on cur
               if rng.random()<0.4:
                   cur=_spectral_candidate(cur,ctx,rng,3)
               q_cycle? no_accept=0; eta=0.12
       it +=1
       if time.monotonic() - last_best > 180:
           # if long no global best, jump
           cur=best_a.copy()
           no_accept=0; eta=0.12
    # final: maybe try best with low-eta few iter? loop already.
    return best_a,best_score
```

Code for `_optimize_ctx`:
```
def _optimize_ctx(a, ctx, rng, limit):
    n,L,m=ctx
    limit=float(limit)
    if limit <=0:
       arr=_normalize(a,1e-15); return arr, _score_ctx(arr,ctx)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+limit
    cur=_normalize(a,1e-15)
    cur_score=_score_ctx(cur,ctx)
    best_a=cur.copy(); best_score=cur_score
    eta=0.08
    no_accept=0
    it=0
    q_cycle=(1,1,2,2,2,4,4,6,8,8,12,16)
    last_best=time.monotonic()
    one_m = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    # maybe precompute? m large, okay.
    while True:
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now > deadline - 1.5: break
       B=_fft_conv_self(cur,L,m)
       mx=float(np.max(B))
       if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <=0: break
       cur_score=float(2.0*n*mx)  # cur normalized
       if cur_score < best_score - 1e-14:
           best_score=cur_score; best_a=cur.copy(); last_best=now; no_accept=0
       if now - last_best > 240.0:
           cur=best_a.copy()
           no_accept=0; eta=0.08
       M=mx
       r=B/M
       q_idx = 0
       if it > 0:
           base = min(len(q_cycle)-1, it//25 + min(7, no_accept//6))
           q_idx=base
       q=int(q_cycle[q_idx])
       if rng.random() < 0.12:
           choices=(1,2,4,8,16)
           q=int(choices[int(rng.integers(0,len(choices)))])
       with np.errstate(under='ignore', over='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
           u=np.power(r, q)
       u=np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, 0.0)
       su=float(np.sum(u))
       if su <=0.0:
           u=one_m.copy()
       else:
           u=u/su
       alpha_bg=0.02
       if q >=4: alpha_bg=0.05
       u = (1.0-alpha_bg)*u + alpha_bg*one_m
       # normalize (one_m sum1)
       u *= 1.0 # Because one_m sum1, weighted sum remains? (1-a)*sum u (=1)+a*1=1. Good.
       if q >= 8:
           threshold=0.88 + 0.06*float(rng.random())
           mask = r >= threshold
           cnt=int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
           if cnt > 0:
               if cnt < m//2:
                   hv=np.zeros(m,dtype=np.float64)
                   hv[mask]=1.0
                   hv /= float(cnt)
                   mix_hard=0.35 if q>=16 else 0.25
                   u = (1.0-mix_hard)*u + mix_hard*hv
                   us=sum; if us>0 u/=us
       g=_gradient_direct(u,cur,n,L,m,True)
       gm=float(np.mean(g))
       if not np.isfinite(gm) or gm <=0:
           break
       best_trial_score=cur_score
       best_trial_vec=cur
       # trials
       for e in (eta, 0.5*eta):
           x=np.log(cur + 1e-300)
           x = x - e * (g / gm)
           mx_x=float(np.max(x))
           if not np.isfinite(mx_x): continue
           x -= mx_x
           y=np.exp(x)
           ys=float(np.sum(y))
           if not np.isfinite(ys) or ys <=0: continue
           y/=ys
           sc=_score_ctx(y,ctx)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_trial_score - 1e-14:
               best_trial_score=sc; best_trial_vec=y
       # inverse
       inv=np.empty(n,dtype=np.float64)
       g_safe=np.maximum(g,1e-300)
       np.divide(gm, g_safe, out=inv)
       np.clip(inv,1e-8,1e6,out=inv)
       for beta in (0.35,0.7):
           factor=np.power(inv,beta)
           y=cur*factor
           ys=float(np.sum(y))
           if not np.isfinite(ys) or ys <=0: continue
           y/=ys
           sc=_score_ctx(y,ctx)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_trial_score - 1e-14:
               best_trial_score=sc; best_trial_vec=y
       # random micro if no improve and remaining
       if best_trial_score >= cur_score - 1e-14:
           if rng.random() < 0.25:
               noise=rng.normal(0.0,0.03,n)
               y=cur*np.exp(noise)
               ys=sum; if ys>0: normalize; sc; update best_trial
       if best_trial_score < cur_score - 1e-14:
           cur=best_trial_vec
           cur_score=best_trial_score
           no_accept=0
           eta=min(0.5, eta*1.15)
       else:
           no_accept +=1
           eta=max(0.01, eta*0.8)
           if no_accept == 25 or no_accept == 55:
               y=_cg_candidate(cur,u,gm,n,L,m,10)
               sc=_score_ctx(y,ctx)
               if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-14:
                   cur=y; cur_score=sc; no_accept=0
           if no_accept >= 70:
               mixf=float(rng.uniform(0.25,0.65))
               style=int(rng.integers(0,3))
               prior=_fourier_prior(n,rng,style)
               cur=_normalize((1.0-mixf)*best_a + mixf*prior, 1e-15)
               if rng.random() < 0.35:
                   cur=_spectral_candidate(cur,ctx,rng,3)
               eta=0.08
               no_accept=0
       it +=1
    # Optionally ensure best score re-evaluated exact
    return best_a,best_score
```

We can write `_top_k_indices(scores,k)`:
```
def _top_k_indices(scores,k):
  idxs=list(range(len(scores)))
  for i in range(len(idxs)):
    best=i
    for j in range(i+1,len(idxs)):
       if scores[idxs[j]] < scores[idxs[best]]: best=j
    idxs[i],idxs[best]=idxs[best],idxs[i]
  return idxs[:k]
```

```
def _sort_candidates_by_score(scored):
    # scored list [ (arr, score), ... ]
    n=len(scored)
    for i in range(n):
       bi=i
       for j in range(i+1,n):
          if scored[j][1] < scored[bi][1]: bi=j
       if bi!=i:
          scored[i],scored[bi]=scored[bi],scored[i]
    return scored
```

Let's implement `_internal_exact` for fallback:
```
def _internal_exact(seq):
   if not isinstance(seq,list) or not seq: return inf
   a=[]
   for x in seq:
      if isinstance(x,bool) or not isinstance(x,(int,float)): return inf
      if np.isnan(x) or np.isinf(x): return inf
      v=float(x)
      if v<0: v=0.0
      elif v>1000: v=1000.0
      a.append(v)
   n=len(a); s=sum(a); if s<0.01: return inf
   if n <= 5000:
      ar=np.asarray(a,float); b=np.convolve(ar,ar); return float(2*n*max(b)/(s*s))
   arr=np.asarray(a,float)
   L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
   c=_fft_conv_self(arr,L,2*n-1)
   return float(2*n*float(np.max(c))/(s*s))
```

```
def _param_candidates(n):
    t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    out=[]
    for a in (-2.0,-1.0,0.0,1.0,2.0):
        for b in (-2.0,-1.0,0.0,1.0,2.0):
            for c in (-1.0,0.0,1.0):
                z=a*(t-0.5)+b*np.sin(2*np.pi*t)+c*np.sin(4*np.pi*t)
                z=np.clip(z - np.max(z), -700.0, 0.0)
                p=np.maximum(np.exp(z),1e-300)
                out.append(_normalize(p,1e-15))
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=np.float64)
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)**2.

The program mainly searches one long discrete sequence (default 18000,
still using a 65536-point FFT), starting from resampled known sequences,
low-order Fourier/power priors, and spectral-root restarts.

The optimizer combines:
  - multiplicative / exponential-gradient updates in log-space,
  - target-aware weight construction for the current max-convolution lags,
  - inverse-gradient candidates,
  - occasional shortest-path inverse attempts on the active Hankel operator,
  - random reheat, spectral projection restarts, and a small candidate pool.

All helpers are top-level; no closures or lambdas are used.
"""

import math
import time
import numpy as np

TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def _ctx_for_n(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 1:
        n = 1
    m = 2 * n - 1
    # Need enough length for a*a and also for gradient matvecs
    # conv(w length 2n-1, a_rev length n) => 3n-2.
    L = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    return (n, L, m)


def _normalize(a, floor=0.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size) if a.size else 1.0
    a = a / s
    if floor > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, floor)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
            a = a / s
    return a


def _fft_conv_self(a, L, m):
    f = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(f * f, L)[:m]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _score_ctx(a, ctx):
    n, L, m = ctx
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size != n:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    c = _fft_conv_self(a, L, m)
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def _gradient_direct(w, p, n, L, m, clip=True):
    # g_i = sum_j w_{i+j} p_j, for i=0..n-1.
    # This is conv(w, p[::-1])[n-1 : 2n-1].
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p_rev, L)
    g = np.fft.irfft(W * P, L)[(n - 1):(2 * n - 1)]
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    if clip:
        np.maximum(g, 0.0, out=g)
    return g


def _gradient_ctx(w, a, ctx, clip=True):
    n, L, m = ctx
    return _gradient_direct(w, a, n, L, m, clip=clip)


def _fourier_prior(n, rng, style=0):
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    x = 2.0 * t - 1.0
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    z += rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, n)
    z += float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)) * x
    z += float(rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6)) * (x * x - 1.0 / 3.0)

    kmax = int(rng.integers(2, 8))
    for k in range(1, kmax + 1):
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.65) / math.sqrt(float(k)))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph)

    style = int(style) % 3
    if style == 1:
        z += float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.10)) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t)
    elif style == 2:
        z += float(rng.uniform(0.20, 0.90)) * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * t)

    z -= float(np.max(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(z)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    return _normalize(p, 1e-15)


def _param_candidates(n):
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    out = []

    # Low-order trigonometric/logistic-looking priors.
    for a in (-2.0, -1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 2.0):
        for b in (-2.0, -1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 2.0):
            for c in (-1.0, 0.0, 1.0):
                z = a * (t - 0.5) + b * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t) + c * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * t)
                z -= float(np.max(z))
                z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
                p = np.exp(z)
                p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
                out.append(_normalize(p, 1e-15))

    # Power-law tails (and reversed versions).
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    for beta in (-2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        z = beta * np.log(idx)
        z -= float(np.max(z))
        z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
        p = np.exp(z)
        p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
        q = _normalize(p, 1e-15)
        out.append(q)
        out.append(q[::-1].copy())

    return out


def _spectral_candidate(a, ctx, rng, iters=3):
    n, L, m = ctx
    a = _normalize(a, 1e-15)

    for _ in range(max(1, int(iters))):
        B = _fft_conv_self(a, L, m)
        mx = float(np.max(B))
        if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
            break

        med = float(np.median(B))
        gamma = float(rng.uniform(0.35, 0.85))
        T = (1.0 - gamma) * B + gamma * med

        # Also compress the upper tail a bit.
        hi = med + float(rng.uniform(0.35, 0.75)) * max(1e-200, mx - med)
        T = np.minimum(T, hi)
        T = np.maximum(T, 0.0)
        ts = float(T.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ts) or ts <= 0.0:
            break
        T = T / ts

        # Spectral square root of the target coefficient sequence.
        Tf = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        Tf[:m] = T
        R = np.sqrt(np.fft.rfft(Tf, L))
        R[0] = complex(float(np.real(R[0])), 0.0)
        R[-1] = complex(float(np.real(R[-1])), 0.0)
        A = np.fft.irfft(R, L)[:n]
        A = np.where(np.isfinite(A), A, 0.0)
        A = np.maximum(A, 0.0)
        if float(A.sum()) <= 1e-250:
            break
        a = _normalize(A, 1e-15)

    return a


def _resample_parent(n):
    par = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if par is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(par, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size <= 1:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    if float(arr.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return None

    if arr.size == n:
        return _normalize(arr, 1e-15)

    xs = np.linspace(0.0, float(arr.size - 1), n, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.arange(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.interp(xs, idx, arr)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return _normalize(out, 1e-15)


def _sort_candidates_by_score(scored):
    # Simple insertion/selection sort to avoid lambda/key callbacks.
    n = len(scored)
    for i in range(n):
        bi = i
        for j in range(i + 1, n):
            if scored[j][1] < scored[bi][1]:
                bi = j
        if bi != i:
            scored[i], scored[bi] = scored[bi], scored[i]
    return scored


def _top_k_indices(scores, k):
    idxs = list(range(len(scores)))
    n = len(idxs)
    k = min(k, n)
    for i in range(k):
        bi = i
        for j in range(i + 1, n):
            if scores[idxs[j]] < scores[idxs[bi]]:
                bi = j
        if bi != i:
            idxs[i], idxs[bi] = idxs[bi], idxs[i]
    return idxs[:k]


def _build_pool(n, ctx, rng, max_pool=12):
    arrs = []

    par = _resample_parent(n)
    if par is not None:
        arrs.append(par)
        for sh in (n // 8, n // 4, n // 2, 3 * n // 4):
            if 0 < sh < n:
                arrs.append(np.roll(par, shift=sh))

    arrs.append(_normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1e-15))
    arrs.extend(_param_candidates(n))

    for i in range(28):
        arrs.append(_fourier_prior(n, rng, style=i % 3))

    scored = []
    for a in arrs:
        a = _normalize(a, 1e-15)
        sc = _score_ctx(a, ctx)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((a, sc))

    if not scored:
        u = _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1e-15)
        sc = _score_ctx(u, ctx)
        scored = [(u, sc if np.isfinite(sc) else 2.0)]

    _sort_candidates_by_score(scored)

    # Spectral-root polish on the best starting vectors.
    top_cnt = min(4, len(scored))
    for i in range(top_cnt):
        a0, _ = scored[i]
        sa = _spectral_candidate(a0, ctx, rng, iters=3)
        ss = _score_ctx(sa, ctx)
        if np.isfinite(ss):
            scored.append((sa, ss))

    _sort_candidates_by_score(scored)

    pool = []
    psc = []
    for a, s in scored[:max_pool]:
        pool.append(a.copy())
        psc.append(float(s))

    if not pool:
        pool.append(_normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1e-15))
        psc.append(2.0)

    return pool, psc


def _add_to_pool(pool, scores, arr, sc, max_len=12):
    if arr is None:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    if len(scores) < max_len:
        pool.append(np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).copy())
        scores.append(float(sc))
        return

    worst = 0
    for i in range(1, len(scores)):
        if scores[i] > scores[worst]:
            worst = i
    if sc < float(scores[worst]):
        pool[worst] = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        scores[worst] = float(sc)


def _cg_candidate(a, w, target_level, n, L, m, iters=10):
    # Solve approximately H_x(a) * z = target_level * 1,
    # where H_x(a)_ij = w_{i+j}, using normal-equation CG.
    if target_level <= 0.0:
        return a

    x = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    Hx = _gradient_direct(w, x, n, L, m, clip=False)
    r = target_level * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) - Hx
    p = r.copy()
    rho = float(np.dot(r, r))
    if not np.isfinite(rho) or rho <= 1e-300:
        return a

    for _ in range(iters):
        y = _gradient_direct(w, p, n, L, m, clip=False)
        py = float(np.dot(p, y))
        if not np.isfinite(py) or py <= 1e-300:
            break
        alpha = rho / py
        if not np.isfinite(alpha):
            break

        x = x + alpha * p
        r = r - alpha * y
        rho_new = float(np.dot(r, r))
        if not np.isfinite(rho_new) or rho_new <= 1e-300:
            break

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)) or np.max(np.abs(x)) > 1e7:
            break

        beta = rho_new / rho
        p = r + beta * p
        rho = rho_new

    y = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(y.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return a
    y = y / s

    # Blend to stay in the same basin unless this is dramatically better.
    return _normalize(0.5 * y + 0.5 * a, 1e-15)


def _optimize_ctx(a, ctx, rng, limit):
    n, L, m = ctx
    limit = float(limit)
    if limit <= 0.1:
        arr = _normalize(a, 1e-15)
        return arr, _score_ctx(arr, ctx)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, limit)
    cur = _normalize(a, 1e-15)
    cur_score = _score_ctx(cur, ctx)
    best_a = cur.copy()
    best_score = cur_score if np.isfinite(cur_score) else float("inf")

    eta = 0.08
    no_accept = 0
    it = 0
    q_cycle = (1, 1, 2, 2, 2, 4, 4, 6, 8, 8, 12, 16)
    last_best = time.monotonic()

    one_m = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    target_m = TARGET_SCORE / (2.0 * float(n))

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 1.2:
            break

        B = _fft_conv_self(cur, L, m)
        mx = float(np.max(B))
        s = float(cur.sum())
        if (not np.isfinite(mx)) or mx <= 0.0 or (not np.isfinite(s)) or s <= 0.0:
            break
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))

        if np.isfinite(cur_score) and cur_score < best_score - 1e-14:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_a = cur.copy()
            last_best = now
            no_accept = 0

        if now - last_best > 260.0:
            cur = best_a.copy()
            no_accept = 0
            eta = 0.08

        # Target-aware reference scaling.  When above the target bound,
        # reference lags near the target level rather than the raw max.
        if cur_score > TARGET_SCORE * 0.998:
            ref = max(0.98 * mx, target_m)
            hard_thr = target_m
        else:
            ref = mx
            hard_thr = 0.90 * mx
        if ref <= 1e-300:
            ref = 1e-300

        r = B / ref
        r = np.minimum(r, 8.0)

        q_base = 0
        if it > 0:
            q_base = min(len(q_cycle) - 1, it // 25 + min(7, no_accept // 6))
        q = int(q_cycle[q_base])
        if rng.random() < 0.12:
            choices = (1, 2, 4, 8, 16)
            q = int(choices[int(rng.integers(0, len(choices)))]))

        with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            u = np.power(r, q)
        u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, 0.0)
        su = float(np.sum(u))
        if su <= 0.0:
            u = one_m.copy()
        else:
            u = u / su

        alpha_bg = 0.02 if q < 4 else 0.05
        u = (1.0 - alpha_bg) * u + alpha_bg * one_m

        if q >= 8:
            mask = B >= hard_thr
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
            if 0 < cnt < m // 2:
                hv = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
                hv[mask] = 1.0
                hv /= float(cnt)
                hard_mix = 0.35 if q >= 16 else 0.25
                u = (1.0 - hard_mix) * u + hard_mix * hv
                us = float(np.sum(u))
                if us > 0.0:
                    u /= us

        g = _gradient_direct(u, cur, n, L, m, clip=True)
        gm = float(np.mean(g))
        if (not np.isfinite(gm)) or gm <= 0.0:
            break

        best_trial_score = cur_score
        best_trial_vec = cur

        # Multiplicative gradient steps.
        for e in (eta, 0.5 * eta):
            x = np.log(cur + 1e-300)
            x = x - e * (g / gm)
            xmax = float(np.max(x))
            if not np.isfinite(xmax):
                continue
            x -= xmax
            x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 0.0)
            y = np.exp(x)
            ys = float(np.sum(y))
            if (not np.isfinite(ys)) or ys <= 0.0:
                continue
            y /= ys
            sc = _score_ctx(y, ctx)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_trial_score - 1e-14:
                best_trial_score = sc
                best_trial_vec = y

        # Inverse-gradient smoothing.
        inv = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        g_safe = np.maximum(g, 1e-300)
        np.divide(gm, g_safe, out=inv)
        np.clip(inv, 1e-8, 1e6, out=inv)
        for beta in (0.35, 0.70):
            factor = np.power(inv, beta)
            y = cur * factor
            ys = float(np.sum(y))
            if (not np.isfinite(ys)) or ys <= 0.0:
                continue
            y /= ys
            sc = _score_ctx(y, ctx)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_trial_score - 1e-14:
                best_trial_score = sc
                best_trial_vec = y

        # Small random nudge if stuck.
        if best_trial_score >= cur_score - 1e-14:
            if rng.random() < 0.25:
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, n)
                y = cur * np.exp(noise)
                y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
                ys = float(np.sum(y))
                if np.isfinite(ys) and ys > 0.0:
                    y /= ys
                    sc = _score_ctx(y, ctx)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_trial_score - 1e-14:
                        best_trial_score = sc
                        best_trial_vec = y

        if best_trial_score < cur_score - 1e-14:
            cur = best_trial_vec
            cur_score = best_trial_score
            no_accept = 0
            eta = min(0.5, eta * 1.15)
            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-14:
                best_score = cur_score
                best_a = cur.copy()
                last_best = time.monotonic()
        else:
            no_accept += 1
            eta = max(0.01, eta * 0.80)

            if no_accept == 25 or no_accept == 55:
                y = _cg_candidate(cur, u, gm, n, L, m, iters=10)
                sc = _score_ctx(y, ctx)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-14:
                    cur = y
                    cur_score = sc
                    no_accept = 0
                    if cur_score < best_score - 1e-14:
                        best_score = cur_score
                        best_a = cur.copy()
                        last_best = time.monotonic()

            if no_accept >= 70:
                mixf = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.65))
                style = int(rng.integers(0, 3))
                prior = _fourier_prior(n, rng, style)
                cur = _normalize((1.0 - mixf) * best_a + mixf * prior, 1e-15)
                if rng.random() < 0.35:
                    cur = _spectral_candidate(cur, ctx, rng, iters=3)
                eta = 0.08
                no_accept = 0

        it += 1

    return best_a, best_score


def _internal_exact(seq):
    if not isinstance(seq, list) or not seq:
        return float("inf")
    a = []
    for x in seq:
        if isinstance(x, bool) or not isinstance(x, (int, float)):
            return float("inf")
        if np.isnan(x) or np.isinf(x):
            return float("inf")
        v = float(x)
        if v < 0.0:
            v = 0.0
        elif v > 1000.0:
            v = 1000.0
        a.append(v)
    n = len(a)
    s = sum(a)
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")

    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 5000:
        b = np.convolve(arr, arr)
        mb = float(max(b))
        return float(2.0 * n * mb / (s * s))

    m = 2 * n - 1
    L = _next_pow2(m)
    c = _fft_conv_self(arr, L, m)
    mb = float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0 * n * mb / (s * s))


def _exact_score(seq):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except NameError:
        val = _internal_exact(seq)
    except Exception:
        val = _internal_exact(seq)

    if val is None:
        return float("inf")

    try:
        val = float(val)
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    return val


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search entry point.  Returns a list of non-negative floats.
    """
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if (not np.isfinite(budget)) or budget <= 1.0:
        budget = 2400.0

    try:
        if "time_limit" in kwargs:
            budget = float(kwargs["time_limit"])
    except Exception:
        pass

    if (not np.isfinite(budget)) or budget <= 1.0:
        budget = 2400.0

    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()

    # Leave room for final exact evaluation and return.
    hard = start + max(20.0, budget - 5.0)
    search_deadline = hard - 120.0
    if search_deadline < start + 5.0:
        search_deadline = start + max(1.0, 0.5 * (hard - start))

    # For normal budgets use a long sequence; for tiny budgets stay modest.
    if budget < 600.0:
        n = 6121
    else:
        n = 18000

    ctx = _ctx_for_n(n)

    print(f"[search] n={n}, budget={budget:.1f}s, ctx={ctx}")
    pool, pool_scores = _build_pool(n, ctx, rng, max_pool=12)
    if not pool:
        pool = [_normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 1e-15)]
        pool_scores = [2.0]

    best_idx = _top_k_indices(pool_scores, 1)[0]
    best_a = pool[best_idx].copy()
    best_score = float(pool_scores[best_idx])
    print(f"[search] initial best internal score={best_score:.9f}")

    last_global = time.monotonic()
    cycle = 0

    while time.monotonic() < search_deadline:
        now = time.monotonic()
        rem = search_deadline - now
        if rem < 15.0:
            break

        slice_len = min(120.0, max(15.0, 0.08 * rem))
        if slice_len > rem - 10.0:
            slice_len = max(5.0, rem - 10.0)

        top_idx = _top_k_indices(pool_scores, 1)[0]
        u = rng.random()
        if u < 0.55:
            idx = top_idx
        elif u < 0.82:
            half = max(1, len(pool_scores) // 2)
            idx = int(rng.integers(0, half))
            if idx >= len(pool_scores):
                idx = 0
        else:
            idx = int(rng.integers(0, len(pool_scores)))

        a0 = pool[idx].copy()
        a_new, sc_new = _optimize_ctx(a0, ctx, rng, slice_len)

        if np.isfinite(sc_new):
            if sc_new < float(pool_scores[idx]) - 1e-14:
                pool[idx] = a_new
                pool_scores[idx] = float(sc_new)

            if sc_new < best_score - 1e-14:
                best_score = float(sc_new)
                best_a = a_new.copy()
                last_global = time.monotonic()
                print(f"[search] cycle {cycle}: improved best to {best_score:.9f}")

        # Inject fresh diversity periodically or when stuck.
        if cycle % 4 == 0 or (time.monotonic() - last_global > 300.0):
            fresh = _fourier_prior(n, rng, style=int(rng.integers(0, 3)))
            if rng.random() < 0.30:
                fresh = _spectral_candidate(fresh, ctx, rng, iters=3)
            fs = _score_ctx(fresh, ctx)
            _add_to_pool(pool, pool_scores, fresh, fs, max_len=12)

        cycle += 1
        if cycle % 5 == 0:
            now_best = _top_k_indices(pool_scores, 1)[0]
            print(f"[search] cycle {cycle}, best internal={best_score:.9f}, "
                  f"pool_best={pool_scores[now_best]:.9f}, time_left={max(0.0, hard - time.monotonic()):.1f}s")

    # Final polish, leaving a strict window for exact evaluation.
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem_polish = hard - now - 80.0
    if rem_polish > 10.0:
        best_a, best_score = _optimize_ctx(best_a, ctx, rng, rem_polish)
        print(f"[search] after final polish best internal={best_score:.9f}")

    # Final exact checks.  Keep this short because np.convolve in evaluate_sequence
    # is direct for large n.
    best_seq = None
    best_exact = float("inf")

    # Evaluate primary candidate.
    seq = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]
    val = _exact_score(seq)
    if np.isfinite(val):
        best_exact = val
        best_seq = seq
    print(f"[search] exact primary={best_exact!r}")

    # Evaluate the provided parent sequence, if present and small enough to be cheap.
    par = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    do_parent = True
    if par is not None:
        try:
            arrp = np.asarray(par, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if time.monotonic() < hard - 30.0 and arrp.size > 0 and arrp.size <= 10000:
                seqp = [float(x) for x in np.maximum(arrp, 0.0)]
                valp = _exact_score(seqp)
                if np.isfinite(valp) and (best_exact == float("inf") or valp < best_exact):
                    best_exact = valp
                    best_seq = seqp
                do_parent = False
        except Exception:
            do_parent = True

    if do_parent and best_seq is None:
        best_seq = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()]

    # Safety: ensure valid list of finite nonnegative floats.
    cleaned = []
    for x in best_seq:
        try:
            y = float(x)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if not np.isfinite(y):
            y = 0.0
        if y < 0.0:
            y = 0.0
        if y > 1000.0:
            y = 1000.0
        cleaned.append(y)

    if not cleaned or sum(cleaned) < 0.01:
        # Fallback: uniform sequence for the searched length.
        cleaned = [1.0 / float(len(cleaned) if cleaned else n) for _ in range(n)]

    print(f"[search] returning length={len(cleaned)}, exact used={best_exact!r}")
    return cleaned
```
